English-Hokkienese Dictionary in the Script of National Language of Swatow dialect

This dictionary is based on English-Chinese Vocabulary of the Vernacular Or Spoken Language of Swatow by Rudolf Lechler, Samuel Wells Williams, William Duffus
auther: Vòng Vân-Hạu

how? țãi-sę? țò-nį̉? zủ-hỏ? how dare! țò-cą́! how many? diẹh-țọi? kúi-kải? diạc-kan? ki-to? how far? diẹh-hững? how many times? diẹh-țọi-ẹ? kúi-hỏe? kúi-pái? kúi-tứng? kúi-hãng? diẹh-țọi-txừ? how large? diẹh-tọa? how old? diẹh-țọi-hòe? txun-chhiu diẹh-țọi? diẹh-tọa? diẹh-lãu? kùi-cę? how to do? țò-nį̉-moẹh? not know how to do it, țò ṃ-pat tò-khừ. how shall I do? zủ-hỏ sĩ-hó? țò-nį̉ chią̀-hó? how is this? xự-sĩ-țò-nį̉? how did it happen? țò-õi? țò-ũ?
